text-basic-primary">Trinity-Large-Thinking: Scaling an Open Source Frontier Agent</h1></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 pb-12 md:pb-16"><div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 md:flex-row md:gap-5"><div class="inline-flex items-start gap-1.5"><div class="flex shrink-0 items-center"><span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full"><div class="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow-convex text-white font-bold overflow-hidden p-[1px] w-5 h-5 min-w-5 min-h-5 max-w-5 max-h-5 text-xs" style="background-color:var(--color-bg-elevation-sublevel-variant-B)"><img src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/qmhg7q8j/prod/6689ee4255c275f2c4fe693a90fdad7caa386dcd-536x510.png?rect=13,0,510,510&amp;w=48&amp;h=48&amp;q=80&amp;auto=format" alt="Lucas Atkins" class="w-full h-full object-cover rounded-full"/></div></span></div><div class="flex items-start"><span class="label-sm max-w-[280px] text-basic-primary">Lucas Atkins</span></div></div><p class="label-sm text-basic-secondary">5 min read • Apr 1, 2026</p></div><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-divider-secondary px-3 py-1.5 uppercase tag-xs text-basic-secondary backdrop-blur-[2px]">News</span></div><div class="flex w-full border-l-4 border-accent-primary pl-4 md:pl-6"><p class="text-bold-lg text-basic-primary md:text-bold-xl">Trinity-Large-Thinking is live. A frontier open reasoning model for complex, long-horizon agents and multi-turn tool calling released under Apache 2.0.</p></div><div class="w-full pt-10"><figure style="--figure-max-width:2400px;--figure-max-width-mobile:2400px" class="flex w-full flex-col gap-1 figure-max-width mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0"><button type="button" aria-label="Open image: Trinity Large Thinking Benchmarks" class="group relative flex w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-[4px] border border-tertiary focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--blue-600)] focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-transparent md:rounded-[8px]"><div class="relative inline-block overflow-hidden w-full transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-[1.02] figure-aspect" style="--figure-aspect:2400 / 1350;--figure-aspect-mobile:2400 / 1350"><div class="block w-full h-full"><img src="data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/2wBDAAYEBQYFBAYGBQYHBwYIChAKCgkJChQODwwQFxQYGBcUFhYaHSUfGhsjHBYWICwgIyYnKSopGR8tMC0oMCUoKSj/2wBDAQcHBwoIChMKChMoGhYaKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCgoKCj/wAARCAALABQDASIAAhEBAxEB/8QAGAAAAwEBAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAMEBQj/xAAgEAACAgICAgMAAAAAAAAAAAABAgADBBESMRMhMjNx/8QAFgEBAQEAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAwEC/8QAFxEAAwEAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAERAv/aAAwDAQACEQMRAD8A57x7Kii7S1jr2RKGya1HHxWg69/syarrETirkDfQjBkW7+xu5qhvGW60Myb0az4uCBo7MJHYxaxix2Se4SCU/9k=" alt="" aria-hidden="true" class="absolute inset-0 block w-full h-full object-cover object-center transition-opacity ease-out opacity-100" style="transition-duration:300ms"/></div></div><span class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-black opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-300 group-hover:opacity-10 group-active:opacity-25"></span></button></figure><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Today we are releasing Trinity-Large-Thinking on our API and the weights on Hugging Face under the Apache 2.0 license.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Nine months ago, we made a decision that changed the shape of this company. We decided that if we cared about serious American open models, about models developers and enterprises could actually own, then we needed to build them ourselves.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">That decision gave us Trinity.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">First came the smaller models, 4.5B, Nano and Mini. Then came Preview at the end of January, our first public look at Trinity Large.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Today comes the official release: Trinity-Large-Thinking, our reasoning model built to close the gaps Preview left open.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">On many axes, it is the strongest open model ever released outside of China.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">It is the result of the last two months spent improving and scaling our SFT and RL pipeline so it could meet the size and capability of the Trinity-Large base model. Preview was an instruct model, as the name would suggest, the new checkpoint uses “thinking” prior to responding, like Trinity-Mini does. This enables stronger multi-turn tool calling, better context coherence, cleaner instruction following, and more stable behavior across long-running agent loops.<br/></p><figure class="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0"><div><video src="https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/kilo_snake.mp4" 
       autoplay loop muted playsinline 
       style="width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);">
</video></div></figure><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">The market has been asking for this. Trinity-Large-Preview took off faster than we expected.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">We launched it at the end of January as a light instruct post-train, with the expectation that people would test it, break it, and help show us where it wanted to go next. That is exactly what happened. The model later crossed just 3.37 trillion tokens served on OpenRouter in its first 2 months, and OpenRouter’s OpenClaw collection has Trinity Large Preview as the #1 most used open model in the U.S. and #4 globally.</p><figure class="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0"><div><video src="https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/hermes_demo.mp4" 
       autoplay loop muted playsinline 
       style="width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);">
</video></div></figure><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">And they hit our GPUs like a freight train, we couldn’t have asked for a better way to stress test our serving platform.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">And because we know our Preview model had a special place in many users’ hearts, we’re keeping it free on OpenRouter, albeit on less hardware. We’ll have more updates on the long-term plans for that model in the coming weeks.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">When approaching Large-Thinking, given the capital we had and the timeline in front of us, we did not think we could become the best open coding model in the world overnight. We also did not think that was the only problem worth solving. What we thought we could do, and what Preview started to prove, was build a model that was unusually good for the kinds of agents developers were starting to run every day, 24/7.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">That meant focusing on the things that make agents feel real in practice: staying coherent across turns, using tools without getting sloppy, following instructions under constraint, and keeping quality high without making the economics absurd.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Trinity-Large-Thinking is the official release of that bet.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">It is available through our API. The weights are available on Hugging Face under Apache 2.0. We are releasing it this way because we continue to believe that permissive American open weights are necessary for the US to fully claim that we’re “leading” in AI.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Developers and Enterprises need models they can inspect, post-train, host, distill, and own.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Trinity-Large-Thinking clears an important bar on both capability and usability.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">It scores #2 on PinchBench, a benchmark from Kilo measuring model capability on tasks relevant to agents like OpenClaw, just behind Opus-4.6 while landing at $0.90 per million output tokens on our API, roughly 96% cheaper.</p><figure class="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0"><div><video src="https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/cline_budget_app.mp4" 
       autoplay loop muted playsinline 
       style="width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);">
</video></div></figure><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">It is far better than Preview at multi-turn tool use, context coherence, and instruction following across long-horizon agent runs. Our goal has been reliable, cheap, high-quality agents for developers, startups, and enterprises. We feel we’ve hit each of these.<br/></p><figure class="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0"><div><video src="https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/thinking-screensaver.mp4" 
       autoplay loop muted playsinline 
       style="width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);">
</video></div></figure><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">The last nine months have been a real sprint to reach the frontier.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">We had to prove we could stand up the full pipeline, make sparsity behave, stabilize training, push post-training harder, and do it all without the luxury of endless capital reserves.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">That constraint shaped the company in meaningful ways: it forced us to be precise about what mattered, pick our battles, and build a training culture that takes efficiency seriously from pretraining through RL.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Standing up our pipeline efficiently meant pretraining on 2048 NVIDIA B300s and handling post-training across 1152 H100s. Today, that same optimization extends to production, where our inference stack is powered by NVIDIA Dynamo, Blackwell Ultra GPUs, and vLLM.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">We have reached a level of competitiveness, and a price per token, that we feel genuinely good about. That means the question changes. It is no longer only whether we can get to the frontier. It is whether we can take what worked here, understand it deeply, and use it to train the best open-weight models in the <strong class="font-bold text-basic-primary">world</strong>.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">It is also the harder question, but one we can’t wait to find the answer to.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Now, much of the work that went into Large will now flow back down the stack.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">We will bring the pretraining and post-training lessons from Trinity-Large into our Mini and Nano models. One of the bitter lessons in training great small models is that you usually need to train a really good big one first. Then you distill. We can’t wait to get to work on Trinity-2-Nano and Mini and put that lesson to good use.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Large will keep getting better too. We’re not done with this generation Trinity-Large, but we are at the point where the project becomes even more ambitious.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0"><strong class="font-bold text-basic-primary">A personal note</strong></p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">I am proud of this team.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Getting here took difficult technical work, hard calls, and more than a few moments where the easy thing would have been to lower the ambition. Nobody did that. They kept pushing.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">I am also grateful to the developers, partners, and early users who put Trinity-Large-Preview into real systems while it was still young enough to embarrass itself. That feedback mattered. It helped shape the model we are releasing today.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">Trinity-Large-Thinking is live now. Sign up via our <a href="http://chat.arcee.ai" class="text-accent-primary underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-accent-secondary [&amp;_*]:!text-inherit">platform</a> or <a href="http://openrouter.ai/arcee-ai" class="text-accent-primary underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-accent-secondary [&amp;_*]:!text-inherit">openrouter</a>.</p><p class="mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0">We are building these models so you can own them.</p></div></div></div></section><section class="relative z-10 w-full bg-elevation-ground pb-16 pt-8 We decided that if we cared about serious American open models, about models developers and enterprises could actually own, then we needed to build them ourselves.\"]}],[\"$\",\"p\",\"d799a93782ba\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"That decision gave us Trinity.\"]}],[\"$\",\"p\",\"66a6bd7d6c26\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"First came the smaller models, 4.5B, Nano and Mini. Then came Preview at the end of January, our first public look at Trinity Large.\"]}],[\"$\",\"p\",\"f65d21ace1bc\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Today comes the official release: Trinity-Large-Thinking, our reasoning model built to close the gaps Preview left open.\"]}],[\"$\",\"p\",\"ecfc3cf4e14f\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"On many axes, it is the strongest open model ever released outside of China.\"]}],[\"$\",\"p\",\"bc894d1c93a6\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"It is the result of the last two months spent improving and scaling our SFT and RL pipeline so it could meet the size and capability of the Trinity-Large base model. Preview was an instruct model, as the name would suggest, the new checkpoint uses “thinking” prior to responding, like Trinity-Mini does. This enables stronger multi-turn tool calling, better context coherence, cleaner instruction following, and more stable behavior across long-running agent loops.\",[\"$\",\"br\",\"node-1\",{}],\"\"]}],[\"$\",\"figure\",\"726f04049a69\",{\"className\":\"flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0\",\"style\":{},\"children\":[[\"$\",\"div\",null,{\"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\":{\"__html\":\"\u003cvideo src=\\\"https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/kilo_snake.mp4\\\" \\n       autoplay loop muted playsinline \\n       style=\\\"width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);\\\"\u003e\\n\u003c/video\u003e\"}}],null]}],[\"$\",\"p\",\"4c879eda2e31\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"The market has been asking for this. text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"We launched it at the end of January as a light instruct post-train, with the expectation that people would test it, break it, and help show us where it wanted to go next. That is exactly what happened. The model later crossed just 3.37 trillion tokens served on OpenRouter in its first 2 months, and OpenRouter’s OpenClaw collection has Trinity Large Preview as the #1 most used open model in the U.S. and #4 globally.\"]}]\n37:[\"$\",\"figure\",\"24d84fbfe0c6\",{\"className\":\"flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0\",\"style\":{},\"children\":[[\"$\",\"div\",null,{\"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\":{\"__html\":\"\u003cvideo src=\\\"https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/hermes_demo.mp4\\\" \\n       autoplay loop muted playsinline \\n       style=\\\"width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);\\\"\u003e\\n\u003c/video\u003e\"}}],null]}]\n38:[\"$\",\"p\",\"7d6dfc3c7439\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"And they hit our GPUs like a freight train, we couldn’t have asked for a better way to stress test our serving platform.\"]}]\n39:[\"$\",\"p\",\"b5e340fed03a\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"And because we know our Preview model had a special place in many users’ hearts, we’re keeping it free on OpenRouter, albeit on less hardware. We’ll have more updates on the long-term plans for that model in the coming weeks.\"]}]\n3a:[\"$\",\"p\",\"7ff17cf81ab9\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"When approaching Large-Thinking, given the capital we had and the timeline in front of us, we did not think we could become the best open coding model in the world overnight. We also did not think that was the only problem worth solving. What we thought we could do, and what Preview started to prove, was build a model that was unusually good for the kinds of agents developers were starting to run every day, 24/7.\"]}]\n3b:[\"$\",\"p\",\"f354a44a3048\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"That meant focusing on the things that make agents feel real in practice: staying coherent across turns, using tools without getting sloppy, following instructions under constraint, and keeping quality high without making the economics absurd.\"]}]\n3c:[\"$\",\"p\",\"ad5d87185ed6\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Trinity-Large-Thinking is the official release of that bet.\"]}]\n3d:[\"$\",\"p\",\"89e06626babd\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"It is available through our API. The weights are available on Hugging Face under Apache 2.0. We are releasing it this way because we continue to believe that permissive American open weights are necessary for the US to fully claim that we’re “leading” in AI.\"]}]\n3e:[\"$\",\"p\",\"a30a5d42b055\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Developers and Enterprises need models they can inspect, post-train, host, distill, and own.\"]}]\n3f:[\"$\",\"p\",\"3b50b8c1f9d8\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Trinity-Large-Thinking clears an important bar on both capability and usability.\"]}]\n40:[\"$\",\"p\",\"13c5b2740fa9\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"It scores #2 on PinchBench, a benchmark from Kilo measuring model capability on tasks relevant to agents like OpenClaw, just behind Opus-4.6 while landing at $0.90 per million output tokens on our API, roughly 96% cheaper.\"]}]\n41:[\"$\",\"figure\",\"c4b9c70bc45d\",{\"className\":\"flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0\",\"style\":{},\"children\":[[\"$\",\"div\",null,{\"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\":{\"__html\":\"\u003cvideo src=\\\"https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/cline_budget_app.mp4\\\" \\n       autoplay loop muted playsinline \\n       style=\\\"width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);\\\"\u003e\\n\u003c/video\u003e\"}}],null]}]\n42:[\"$\",\"p\",\"ad9ce0337833\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"It is far better than Preview at multi-turn tool use, context coherence, and instruction following across long-horizon agent runs. Our goal has been reliable, cheap, high-quality agents for developers, startups, and enterprises. We feel we’ve hit each of these.\",[\"$\",\"br\",\"node-1\",{}],\"\"]}]\n43:[\"$\",\"figure\",\"68fb46390f1d\",{\"className\":\"flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8 first:mt-0 mb-8 last:mb-0\",\"style\":{},\"children\":[[\"$\",\"div\",null,{\"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\":{\"__html\":\"\u003cvideo src=\\\"https://arcee-vids.b-cdn.net/thinking-screensaver.mp4\\\" \\n       autoplay loop muted playsinline \\n       style=\\\"width: 100%; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);\\\"\u003e\\n\u003c/video\u003e\"}}],null]}]\n44:[\"$\",\"p\",\"38f8f5477abb\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"The last nine months have been a real sprint to reach the frontier.\"]}]\n45:[\"$\",\"p\",\"ae6059113db5\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"We had to prove we could stand up the full pipeline, make sparsity behave, stabilize training, push post-training harder, and do it all without the luxury of endless capital reserves.\"]}]\n46:[\"$\",\"p\",\"bb8658283950\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"That constraint shaped the company in meaningful ways: it forced us to be precise about what mattered, pick our battles, and build a training culture that takes efficiency seriously from pretraining through RL.\"]}]\n47:[\"$\",\"p\",\"b5c3ef327ff1\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Standing up our pipeline efficiently meant pretraining on 2048 NVIDIA B300s and handling post-training across 1152 H100s. Today, that same optimization extends to production, where our inference stack is powered by NVIDIA Dynamo, Blackwell Ultra GPUs, and vLLM.\"]}]\n48:[\"$\",\"p\",\"9c810553e0e8\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"We have reached a level of competitiveness, and a price per token, that we feel genuinely good about. That means the question changes. It is no longer only whether we can get to the frontier. It is whether we can take what worked here, understand it deeply, and use it to train the best open-weight models in the \",[\"$\",\"strong\",\"ccbfbe08d804\",{\"className\":\"font-bold text-basic-primary\",\"children\":[\"world\"]}],\".\"]}]\n49:[\"$\",\"p\",\"6fd0b510db9a\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"It is also the harder question, but one we can’t wait to find the answer to.\"]}]\n4a:[\"$\",\"p\",\"2f99dd1c3397\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Now, much of the work that went into Large will now flow back down the stack.\"]}]\n4b:[\"$\",\"p\",\"ae7c260e5621\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"We will bring the pretraining and post-training lessons from Trinity-Large into our Mini and Nano models. One of the bitter lessons in training great small models is that you usually need to train a really good big one first. Then you distill. We can’t wait to get to work on Trinity-2-Nano and Mini and put that lesson to good use.\"]}]\n4c:[\"$\",\"p\",\"01bcbffc4a2d\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Large will keep getting better too. We’re not done with this generation Trinity-Large, but we are at the point where the project becomes even more ambitious.\"]}]\n4d:[\"$\",\"p\",\"d4a2365d12bc\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[[\"$\",\"strong\",\"4450e7b797c5\",{\"className\":\"font-bold text-basic-primary\",\"children\":[\"A personal note\"]}]]}]\n4e:[\"$\",\"p\",\"f7a0e0c4a219\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"I am proud of this team.\"]}]\n4f:[\"$\",\"p\",\"b1df88fc072b\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Getting here took difficult technical work, hard calls, and more than a few moments where the easy thing would have been to lower the ambition. Nobody did that. They kept pushing.\"]}]\n50:[\"$\",\"p\",\"a61cfd1347d4\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"I am also grateful to the developers, partners, and early users who put Trinity-Large-Preview into real systems while it was still young enough to embarrass itself. That feedback mattered. It helped shape the model we are releasing today.\"]}]\n51:[\"$\",\"p\",\"052a16b022a6\",{\"className\":\"mt-4 text-md text-basic-secondary first:mt-0 mb-4 last:mb-0\",\"children\":[\"Trinity-Large-Thinking is live now.
